OBJECT Page 5447301 Webshop Shop Card
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=28.07.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    CaptionML=DEU=Webshopkarte;
    SourceTable=Table5447301;
    PageType=Card;
    PromotedActionCategoriesML=[DEU=Neu,Vorgang,Bericht,Aktualisierung;
                                ENU=New,Process,Report,Update];
    OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                       CustomerLinkVisible := NOT ("Shop Typ"="Shop Typ"::b2c);
                       SalespersonLinkVisible := ("Shop Typ"="Shop Typ"::salesperson);

                       AmazonFieldsVisible := ("Marketplace Type" = "Marketplace Type"::Amazon);
                       EbayFieldsVisible := ("Marketplace Type" = "Marketplace Type"::eBay);

                       MarketplaceVisible := ("Shop Typ" = "Shop Typ"::marketplace);

                       CALCFIELDS("Ebay Auth Token");
                       "Ebay Auth Token".CREATEINSTREAM(IStream);
                       EbayAuthTokenBigText.READ(IStream);
                       EbayAuthTokenBigText.GETSUBTEXT(EbayAuthToken,1);
                     END;

    ActionList=ACTIONS
    {
      { 1000000057;  ;ActionContainer;
                      Name=<Action1000000057>;
                      ActionContainerType=ActionItems }
      { 1000000060;1 ;Action    ;
                      Name=<Action1000000060>;
                      CaptionML=[DEU=Webshop �ffnen;
                                 ENU=Open Webshop];
                      Promoted=Yes;
                      PromotedIsBig=Yes;
                      Image=DocInBrowser;
                      PromotedCategory=Process;
                      OnAction=VAR
                                 FrontendLink@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447308;
                               BEGIN
                                 FrontendLink.SetShopCode(Code);
                                 FrontendLink.RUN;
                               END;
                                }
      { 1000000058;1 ;Action    ;
                      Name=<Action1000000058>;
                      CaptionML=[DEU=Grundeinrichtung Aktualisieren;
                                 ENU=Update Setup];
                      Promoted=Yes;
                      PromotedIsBig=Yes;
                      Image=Refresh;
                      PromotedCategory=Process;
                      OnAction=VAR
                                 Text002@1000000000 : TextConst 'DEU=Webshop Einrichtung aktualisieren';
                                 CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean;
                                 i@1000000001 : Integer;
                               BEGIN
                                 IF WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest THEN BEGIN
                                   WebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text002);
                                   CountRecords := TRUE;
                                   WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
                                   FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO BEGIN
                                     WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllWebshopSetup(CountRecords);
                                     WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllShop(CountRecords);
                                     WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllLanguage(CountRecords);
                                     WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllCountry(CountRecords);
                                     WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllShippingOptions(CountRecords);
                                     WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllPaymentOptions(CountRecords);
                                     CountRecords := FALSE;
                                   END;
                                   WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
                                   WebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorClose();
                                 END;
                               END;
                                }
      { 1000000062;1 ;Action    ;
                      Name=<Action1000000010>;
                      CaptionML=[DEU=Update Bewegungsdaten;
                                 ENU=Update Dynamic Data];
                      Promoted=Yes;
                      PromotedIsBig=Yes;
                      Image=Refresh;
                      PromotedCategory=Process;
                      OnAction=VAR
                                 WebshopUpdateDynamicData@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447304;
                               BEGIN
                                 WebshopUpdateDynamicData.UpdateDynamicData;
                               END;
                                }
      { 1000000061;1 ;Action    ;
                      Name=<Action1000000011>;
                      CaptionML=[DEU=Update Gesamtdaten;
                                 ENU=Update All Data];
                      Promoted=Yes;

  {
    VAR
      WebshopManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447301;
      CustomerLinkVisible@1000 : Boolean;
      SalespersonLinkVisible@1001 : Boolean;
      NoSeries@1000000002 : Record 308;
      UseOnlineOrderNoPossible@1000000003 : Boolean;
      UseOnlineOrderNoError@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      Text001@1000000005 : TextConst 'ENU=Biite aktivieren Sie "Manuelle Nr." in der Nummernserie "%1".';
      Text002@1000000006 : TextConst 'ENU=Nummernserie "%1" nicht gefunden.';
      Text003@1000000007 : TextConst 'ENU=Biite aktivieren Sie "Manuelle Nr." in der Nummernserie "%1".';
      Text004@1000000008 : TextConst 'ENU=Keine Standardnummernserie definiert.';
      SalesSetup@1000000009 : Record 311;
      AmazonFieldsVisible@1000000010 : Boolean;
      EbayFieldsVisible@1000000011 : Boolean;
      MarketplaceVisible@1000000012 : Boolean;
      WebshopWebform@1000000016 : Page 5447366;
      Type@1000000015 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2';
      WebshopShop@1000000013 : Record 5447301;
      Text007@1000000019 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte zuerst das n�tige Steuerelement installieren.;ENU=Please install needed Client AddIn first';
      WebformType@1000000020 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection,ShippingOption,PaymentOption,ShippingOptionIcon,PaymentOptionIcon,Marketplace';
      EbayAuthToken@1000000014 : Text[1024];
      EbayAuthTokenBigText@1000000022 : BigText;
      OStream@1000000021 : OutStream;
      IStream@1000000023 : InStream;
      Text008@1000000025 : TextConst 'DEU=Anfrage wird verarbeitet...;ENU=Request is processed';
      Text009@1000000024 : TextConst 'DEU=Kategorien werden abgeholt;ENU=Categories are being fetched';

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckUseOnlineOrderNo@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := FALSE;
      UseOnlineOrderNoError := '';
      SalesSetup.GET;

      IF "Weborder Typ" = "Weborder Typ"::Quote THEN BEGIN
        IF "Quote No. Series Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NoSeries.GET("Quote No. Series Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF NoSeries."Manual Nos." THEN
              UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := TRUE
            ELSE
              UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,FORMAT("Quote No. Series Code"));
          END ELSE
            UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,FORMAT("Quote No. Series Code"));
        END ELSE BEGIN
          NoSeries.SETRANGE(NoSeries.Code,SalesSetup."Quote Nos.");
          IF NoSeries.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF NoSeries."Manual Nos." THEN
              UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := TRUE
            ELSE
              UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text003,FORMAT(SalesSetup."Quote Nos."));
          END ELSE
            UseOnlineOrderNoError := Text004;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF "Order No. Series Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NoSeries.GET("Order No. Series Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF NoSeries."Manual Nos." THEN
              UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := TRUE
            ELSE
              UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,FORMAT("Order No. Series Code"));
          END ELSE
            UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,FORMAT("Order No. Series Code"));
        END ELSE BEGIN
          NoSeries.SETRANGE(NoSeries.Code,SalesSetup."Order Nos.");
          IF NoSeries.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF NoSeries."Manual Nos." THEN
              UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := TRUE
            ELSE
              UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text003,FORMAT(SalesSetup."Order Nos."));
          END ELSE
            UseOnlineOrderNoError := Text004;
        END;
      END;

      IF (NOT (UseOnlineOrderNoPossible)) AND ("Use Online Order No.") THEN
        ERROR(UseOnlineOrderNoError);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }

                           END;
                         END;

                      CaptionML=[DEU=Marktplatz: Unterkategorien abhol.;
                                 ENU=Marketplace: Get Subcategories];
                      Image=GetLines;
                      OnAction=VAR
                                 ParentCodeLineNo@1000000000 : Integer;
                                 Category@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
                                 Window@1000000003 : Dialog;
                                 Factor@1000000004 : Decimal;
                                 Counter@1000000005 : Decimal;
                                 ExitLoop@1000000006 : Boolean;
                               BEGIN
                                 IF Shop.GET("Shop Code") THEN
                                   WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShop(Shop);

                                 IF Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code") THEN
                                   WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateLanguage(Language);

                                 Factor := 9999 / 100;
                                 ExitLoop := FALSE;
                                 Counter := 0;
                                 WebshopMarketplaceManagement.QueueMktplSubCategoriesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type","Search Code");
                                 Window.OPEN(
                                   '#1#################################\\' +
                                   '@2@@@@@@@@');
                                 Window.UPDATE(1, Text008);

                                 REPEAT
                                   Counter += 100;
                                   Window.UPDATE(2, Counter);
                                   IF Counter = 10000 THEN BEGIN
                                     Counter := 0;
                                     Window.UPDATE(2, Counter);
                                   END;
                                   ExitLoop := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.CheckMktplSubCategoriesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
                                 "Search Code");
                                 UNTIL ExitLoop = TRUE;
                                 Window.CLOSE;

                                 ParentCodeLineNo := Category.GetLineNoForSearchCode("Shop Code","Language Code","Search Code");
                                 WebshopMarketplaceManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text009);
                                 WebshopMarketplaceManagement.GetAllFetchedMktplCategories("Shop Code","Language Code",TRUE,0);
                                 WebshopMarketplaceManagement.GetAllFetchedMktplCategories("Shop Code","Language Code",FALSE,ParentCodeLineNo);
                                 WebshopMarketplaceManagement.StateIndicatorClose();

                                 CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
                               END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ShowCampainItemsOnAfterValidat@19002316();
    BEGIN
      "No of Campain ItemsEnable" := "Show Campain Items" <> "Show Campain Items"::None;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ShowRandomItemsOnAfterValidate@19030749();
    BEGIN
      "No of Random ItemsEnable" := "Show Random Items" <> "Show Random Items"::None;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE OnAfterGetCurrRecord@19077479();
    BEGIN
      xRec := Rec;
      "No of Campain ItemsEnable" := "Show Campain Items" <> "Show All Items"::None;
      "No of Campain ItemsEnable" := "Show Campain Items" <> "Show Campain Items"::None;
      "No of Random ItemsEnable" := "Show Random Items" <> "Show Random Items"::None;
    END;

    PROCEDURE OnFetchMarketplaceAttributes@1000000000();
    VAR
      Window@1000000004 : Dialog;
      Factor@1000000003 : Decimal;
      Counter@1000000002 : Decimal;
      ExitLoop@1000000001 : Boolean;
      EntryId@1000000000 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF Shop.GET("Shop Code") THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShop(Shop);

      IF Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code") THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateLanguage(Language);

      Factor := 9999 / 100;
      ExitLoop := FALSE;
      Counter := 0;
      IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
        EntryId := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.QueueMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
      "Marketplace Root Category Code")
      ELSE IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
        EntryId := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.QueueMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
      "Search Code")
      ELSE
        EXIT;
      Window.OPEN(
        '#1#################################\\' +
        '@2@@@@@@@@');
      Window.UPDATE(1, Text008);

      REPEAT
        Counter += 100;
        Window.UPDATE(2, Counter);
        IF Counter = 10000 THEN BEGIN
          Counter := 0;
          Window.UPDATE(2, Counter);
        END;
        IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
          ExitLoop := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.CheckMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
      "Marketplace Root Category Code",EntryId)
        ELSE IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
          ExitLoop := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.CheckMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
      "Search Code",EntryId);
      UNTIL ExitLoop = TRUE;
      Window.CLOSE;
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text010);
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement.FetchCategoryAttributes(Rec,TRUE);
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement.FetchCategoryAttributes(Rec,FALSE);
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement.StateIndicatorClose();
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    LOCAL PROCEDURE FormatName@19039177();
    VAR
      TempLevel@1000000000 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      FormatedName := '';
      NameEmphasize := Level = 1;
      IF Level > 1 THEN BEGIN
        TempLevel := 2;
        REPEAT
          FormatedName += '   ';
          TempLevel := TempLevel + 1;
        UNTIL TempLevel > (Level);
      END;
      FormatedName += Name;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SwapCategory@1000000004(ToCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      TempCategory@1000000008 : Record 5447303;
      TempItemCategory@1000000006 : Record 5447305;
      SwapToCategory@1000000005 : TEMPORARY Record 5447303;
      SwapFromCategory@1000000004 : TEMPORARY Record 5447303;
      SwapToItemCategory@1000000003 : TEMPORARY Record 5447305;
      SwapFromItemCategory@1000000002 : TEMPORARY Record 5447305;
      SwapFromWebshopItem@1000000009 : TEMPORARY Record 5447304;
      SwapToWebshopItem@1000000010 : TEMPORARY Record 5447304;
      WebshopItem@1000000011 : Record 5447304;
      SwapFromAttributeLink@1000000012 : TEMPORARY Record 5447321;
      SwapToAttributeLink@1000000013 : TEMPORARY Record 5447321;
      AttributeLink@1000000014 : Record 5447321;
      SwapToLineNoChildren@1000000015 : Integer;
      SwapFromLineNoChildren@1000000007 : Integer;
      SwapToLineNo@1000000000 : Integer;
      SwapFromLineNo@1000000016 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // Kategorien nach oben oder unter verschieben

      SwapFromLineNo := "Line No.";
      SwapToLineNo := ToCategory."Line No.";

      //Artikel-Hauptkategorien �ndern
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Main Category Line No.",SwapFromLineNo);
      IF WebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapFromWebshopItem.INIT;
        SwapFromWebshopItem.TRANSFERFIELDS(WebshopItem);
        SwapFromWebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
        SwapFromWebshopItem.INSERT(FALSE);
      UNTIL WebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      WebshopItem.INIT;
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Main Category Line No.",SwapToLineNo);
      IF WebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapToWebshopItem.INIT;
        SwapToWebshopItem.TRANSFERFIELDS(WebshopItem);
        SwapToWebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
        SwapToWebshopItem.INSERT(FALSE);
      UNTIL WebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      IF SwapFromWebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
      IF WebshopItem.GET(SwapFromWebshopItem."Shop Code",SwapFromWebshopItem."Language Code",SwapFromWebshopItem."Item No.") THEN BEGIN
        WebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapFromWebshopItem."Main Category Line No.";
        WebshopItem.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
      UNTIL SwapFromWebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      IF SwapToWebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
      IF WebshopItem.GET(SwapToWebshopItem."Shop Code",SwapToWebshopItem."Language Code",SwapToWebshopItem."Item No.") THEN BEGIN
        WebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapToWebshopItem."Main Category Line No.";
        WebshopItem.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
      UNTIL SwapToWebshopItem.NEXT = 0;


      // Kateogorie-Attribute �ber temp. Tabellen verschieben
      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapFromLineNo);
      IF AttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapFromAttributeLink.INIT;
        SwapFromAttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(AttributeLink);
        SwapFromAttributeLink."Category Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
        SwapFromAttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapToLineNo);
      IF AttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapToAttributeLink.INIT;
        SwapToAttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(AttributeLink);
        SwapToAttributeLink."Category Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
        SwapToAttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETFILTER("Category Line No.",'%1|%2',SwapFromLineNo,SwapToLineNo);
      AttributeLink.DELETEALL(FALSE);

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      IF SwapFromAttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        AttributeLink.INIT;
        AttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapFromAttributeLink);
        AttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL SwapFromAttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      IF SwapToAttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        AttributeLink.INIT;
        AttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapToAttributeLink);
        AttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL SwapToAttributeLink.NEXT = 0;


      // Gew�hlte Kategorie in tempor�re Tabelle schreiben und l�schen
      SwapFromCategory.INIT;
      SwapFromCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(Rec);
      SwapFromCategory."Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
      SwapFromCategory.INSERT(FALSE);

      IF Shop.GET("Shop Code") THEN BEGIN

        IF Shop."Use Items from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code",Shop."Use Items from Shop Code")
        ELSE
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");

        IF Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code",Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code")
        ELSE
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code","Shop Code");

        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Language Code","Language Code");
        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapFromLineNo);
        IF TempItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          SwapFromItemCategory.INIT;
          SwapFromItemCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(TempItemCategory);
          SwapFromItemCategory."Category Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
          SwapFromItemCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
          TempItemCategory.DELETE(FALSE);
        UNTIL TempItemCategory.NEXT = 0;
        DELETE(FALSE);

        // Zu tauschende Kategorie in tempor�re Tabelle schreiben und l�schen
        SwapToCategory.INIT;
        SwapToCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(ToCategory);
        SwapToCategory."Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
        SwapToCategory.INSERT(FALSE);

        IF Shop."Use Items from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code",Shop."Use Items from Shop Code")
        ELSE
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");

        IF Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code",Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code")
        ELSE
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code","Shop Code");


        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Language Code","Language Code");
        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapToLineNo);
        IF TempItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          SwapToItemCategory.INIT;
          SwapToItemCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(TempItemCategory);
          SwapToItemCategory."Category Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
          SwapToItemCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
          TempItemCategory.DELETE(FALSE);
        UNTIL TempItemCategory.NEXT = 0;
        ToCategory.DELETE(FALSE);

        // Gew�hlte Kategorie mit neuer Zeilennr. anlegen
        TempCategory.INIT;
        TempCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapFromCategory);
        TempCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
        IF SwapFromItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          TempItemCategory.INIT;
          TempItemCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapFromItemCategory);
          TempItemCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL SwapFromItemCategory.NEXT = 0;

        TempCategory.ModifyChildren;

        // Zu tauschende Kategorie mit neuer Zeilennr. anlegen
        TempCategory.INIT;
        TempCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapToCategory);
        TempCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
        IF SwapToItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          TempItemCategory.INIT;
          TempItemCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapToItemCategory);
          TempItemCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL SwapToItemCategory.NEXT = 0;

        TempCategory.ModifyChildren;

        // Datens�tze im Web tauschen
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SwapCategory("Shop Code","Language Code",SwapFromLineNo,SwapToLineNo);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);

        //Berechtigungen NAV und WEB tauschen
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SwapCategoryPermissions("Shop Code","Language Code",SwapFromLineNo,SwapToLineNo);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);

        COMMIT;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE OnFetchMarketplaceAttributes@1000000000();
    VAR
      Window@1000000004 : Dialog;
      Factor@1000000003 : Decimal;
      Counter@1000000002 : Decimal;
      ExitLoop@1000000001 : Boolean;
      EntryId@1000000000 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF Shop.GET("Shop Code") THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShop(Shop);

      IF Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code") THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateLanguage(Language);

      Factor := 9999 / 100;
      ExitLoop := FALSE;
      Counter := 0;
      IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
        EntryId := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.QueueMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
      "Marketplace Root Category Code")
      ELSE IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
        EntryId := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.QueueMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
      "Search Code")
      ELSE
        EXIT;
      Window.OPEN(
        '#1#################################\\' +
        '@2@@@@@@@@');
      Window.UPDATE(1, Text008);

      REPEAT
        Counter += 100;
        Window.UPDATE(2, Counter);
        IF Counter = 10000 THEN BEGIN
          Counter := 0;
          Window.UPDATE(2, Counter);
        END;
        IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
          ExitLoop := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.CheckMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
      "Marketplace Root Category Code",EntryId)
        ELSE IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
          ExitLoop := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.CheckMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
      "Search Code",EntryId);
      UNTIL ExitLoop = TRUE;
      Window.CLOSE;
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text010);
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement.FetchCategoryAttributes(Rec,TRUE);
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement.FetchCategoryAttributes(Rec,FALSE);
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement.StateIndicatorClose();
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    LOCAL PROCEDURE NameOnFormat@19039177(Text@19045581 : Text[250]) : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      NameEmphasize := (Level = 1);

      IF Level > 1 THEN BEGIN
        TempLevel := 2;
        REPEAT
          Text := '    ' + Text;
          TempLevel := TempLevel + 1
        UNTIL TempLevel > (Level);
      END;

      EXIT(Text);
    END;

    }

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Webshop-Artikel %1 aktualisiert';
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000005 : Codeunit 5447301;
      Search@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      VariantDescriptionIsEditable@1000000003 : Boolean INDATASET;
      ShopFilter@1000000004 : Code[10];
      LanguageFilter@1000000006 : Code[10];
      WebshopSetup@1000000007 : Record 5447300;
      Shop@1000000008 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000009 : Record 5447302;
      ShowAvailableDefaultYes@1000000011 : Boolean;
      ShowAvailableDefaultNo@1000000010 : Boolean;
      ShowAvailableDefault@1000000012 : Boolean;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopFilter@1000000002();
    VAR
      ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      // Shop ermitteln
      IF NOT Shop.GET(ShopFilter) THEN BEGIN
        ShopFilter := '';
        IF GETFILTER("Shop Code") <> '' THEN
          IF Shop.GET(GETFILTER("Shop Code")) THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        IF (ShopFilter = '') AND (WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" <> '') THEN
          IF Shop.GET(WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code") THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        IF ShopFilter = '' THEN
          IF Shop.FINDFIRST THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
      END;

      // Sprache ermitteln
      IF ShopFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT Language.GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter) THEN BEGIN
          LanguageFilter := '';
          Language.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
          IF GETFILTER("Language Code") <> '' THEN
            IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,GETFILTER("Language Code")) THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          IF (LanguageFilter = '') AND (Shop."Default Language Code" <> '') THEN
            IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,Shop."Default Language Code") THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          IF LanguageFilter = '' THEN
            IF Language.FINDFIRST THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
        END;

        // Filter f�r Shop und Sprache setzten
        IF LanguageFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
          FILTERGROUP(4);
          SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
          SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageFilter);
          FILTERGROUP(0);

          // Datensatz anlegen falls nicht vorhanden
          IF "Item No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            ItemNo := "Item No."
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF GETFILTER("Item No.") <> '' THEN
              ItemNo := GETFILTER("Item No.");
          END;
          IF ItemNo <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter,ItemNo) THEN BEGIN
              INIT;
              VALIDATE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
              VALIDATE("Language Code",LanguageFilter);
              VALIDATE("Item No.",ItemNo);
              INSERT(TRUE);
            END;
          END;
          CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE EnableShowAvailable@4();
    VAR
      WebshopShop@1000 : Record 5447301;
    BEGIN
      IF WebshopShop.GET("Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
        CASE WebshopShop."Items Always Available" OF
          WebshopShop."Items Always Available"::Default:
            BEGIN
              ShowAvailableDefault := TRUE;
              ShowAvailableDefaultYes := NOT ShowAvailableDefault;
              ShowAvailableDefaultNo := NOT ShowAvailableDefault;
            END;
          WebshopShop."Items Always Available"::Yes:
            BEGIN
              ShowAvailableDefaultYes := TRUE;
              ShowAvailableDefault := NOT ShowAvailableDefaultYes;
              ShowAvailableDefaultNo := NOT ShowAvailableDefaultYes;
            END;
          WebshopShop."Items Always Available"::No:
            BEGIN
              ShowAvailableDefaultNo := TRUE;
              ShowAvailableDefault := NOT ShowAvailableDefaultNo;
              ShowAvailableDefaultYes := NOT ShowAvailableDefaultNo;
            END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

  {
    VAR
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Artikel %1 Online aktualisiert';
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000009 : Codeunit 5447301;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000002 : Record 5447308;
      WebshopItemFile@1000000003 : Record 5447307;
      ShopFilter@1000000008 : Code[10];
      LanguageFilter@1000000007 : Code[10];
      WebshopSetup@1000000006 : Record 5447300;
      Shop@1000000005 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000004 : Record 5447302;
      LastItemNo@1000000010 : Code[20];
      DoFilter@1000000011 : Boolean;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopFilter@1000000002();
    VAR
      ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      // Shop ermitteln
      IF DoFilter THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT Shop.GET(ShopFilter) THEN BEGIN
          ShopFilter := '';
          IF GETFILTER("Shop Code") <> '' THEN
            IF Shop.GET(GETFILTER("Shop Code")) THEN
              ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
          IF (ShopFilter = '') AND (WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" <> '') THEN
            IF Shop.GET(WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code") THEN
              ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
          IF ShopFilter = '' THEN
            IF Shop.FINDFIRST THEN
              ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        END;

        // Sprache ermitteln
        IF ShopFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT Language.GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter) THEN BEGIN
            LanguageFilter := '';
            Language.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
            IF GETFILTER("Language Code") <> '' THEN
              IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,GETFILTER("Language Code")) THEN
                LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
            IF (LanguageFilter = '') AND (Shop."Default Language Code" <> '') THEN
              IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,Shop."Default Language Code") THEN
                LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
            IF LanguageFilter = '' THEN
              IF Language.FINDFIRST THEN
                LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          END;

          // Filter f�r Shop und Sprache setzten
          IF LanguageFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
            FILTERGROUP(4);
            SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
            SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageFilter);
            FILTERGROUP(0);
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetDoFilter@1000000000(SetTo@1000000000 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      DoFilter := SetTo;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

      Text007@1000000015 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte zuerst das n�tige Steuerelement installieren.;ENU=Please install needed Client AddIn first';

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000005();
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");

      //Webshopartikel aktualisieren (f�r intelligente Suche)
      WebshopItem.GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.");
      IF ((WebshopItem."Last Date Modified" > WebshopItem."Last Date Updated") OR (WebshopItem."Last Date Updated" = 0DT)) THEN BEGIN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateWebshopItemManually(WebshopItem);
        GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");
      END;

      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateWebshopItemDescription(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");
      COMMIT;

      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::ItemDesc);
      WebshopWebform.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetItemNo("Item No.");
      WebshopWebform.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebform.SetRecordCode(Description);
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckWebshopItemDescription(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");

      IF Content THEN
        ContentText := YesText
      ELSE
        ContentText := NoText;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckWebshopItemDescription(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");

      IF Content THEN
        ContentText := YesText
      ELSE
        ContentText := NoText;
    END;

  {
    VAR
      ContentText@1000000001 : Text[30];
      YesText@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Ja';
      NoText@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Nein';
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      TempTextModule@1000000004 : Record 5447309;
      EditTextModule@1000000005 : Page 5447363;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000006 : Codeunit 5447301;
      EditTextModuleAtt@1000000008 : Page 5447365;
      FileText1@1000000009 : Text[30];
      FileText2@1000000007 : Text[30];
      WebshopWebform@1000000012 : Page 5447366;
      Type@1000000011 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2';
      WebformType@1000000010 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection';
      Text007@1000000015 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte zuerst das n�tige Steuerelement installieren.;ENU=Please install needed Client AddIn first';

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateTextModule(Rec);
      GET(Code);
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::TextModul);
      WebshopWebform.SetRecordCode(Code);
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckTextModule(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      GET(Code);

      IF Content THEN
        ContentText := YesText
      ELSE
        ContentText := NoText;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditAttContent@1000000001(AttachmentNo@1000000000 : Integer);
    BEGIN
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateTextModule(Rec);
      GET(Code);
      COMMIT;

      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::TxtModAttac);
      WebshopWebform.SetRecordCode(Code);
      WebshopWebform.SetAttachmentNo(AttachmentNo);
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckTextModuleAttachment(Rec,AttachmentNo);
      GET(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);

      IF "Attachment 1"  THEN
        FileText1 := YesText
      ELSE
        FileText1 := NoText;

      IF "Attachment 2"  THEN
        FileText2 := YesText
      ELSE
        FileText2 := NoText;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE DeleteAttContent@1000000007(AttachmentNo@1000000000 : Integer);
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateTextModule(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteTextModuleAttachment(Rec,1);
      GET(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckTextModuleAttachment(Rec,1);
      GET(Code);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

  {
    VAR
      IconText@1000000000 : Text[10];

      NoText@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Nein';
      WebshopManagement@1000000005 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447301;
      WebshopWebform@1000000007 : Page 5447366;
      Type@1000000006 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2';
      WebformType@1000000004 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection';
      Text007@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte zuerst das n�tige Steuerelement installieren.;ENU=Please install needed Client AddIn first';

    PROCEDURE MoveUp@1000000002();
    VAR
      ToAttributeOption@1000000002 : Record 5447322;
      FromSorting@1000000000 : Integer;
      ToSorting@1000000001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
      ToAttributeOption.SETRANGE("Attribute Code","Attribute Code");
      ToAttributeOption.SETFILTER(Sorting,'<%1',Sorting);
      IF ToAttributeOption.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
        FromSorting := Sorting;
        ToSorting := ToAttributeOption.Sorting;
        Sorting := ToSorting;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
        ToAttributeOption.Sorting := FromSorting;
        ToAttributeOption.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;
      CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE MoveDown@1000000001();
    VAR
      ToAttributeOption@1000000002 : Record 5447322;
      FromSorting@1000000000 : Integer;
      ToSorting@1000000001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
      ToAttributeOption.SETRANGE("Attribute Code","Attribute Code");
      ToAttributeOption.SETFILTER(Sorting,'>%1',Sorting);
      IF ToAttributeOption.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        FromSorting := Sorting;
        ToSorting := ToAttributeOption.Sorting;
        Sorting := ToSorting;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
        ToAttributeOption.Sorting := FromSorting;
        ToAttributeOption.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;
      CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000003();
    VAR
      EditAttributeIcon@1000000001 : Page 5447327;
      TempAttributeOption@1000000000 : Record 5447322;
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest();
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAttributeOption(Rec);
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::AttributeIcon);
      WebshopWebform.SetRecordCode(Code);
      WebshopWebform.SetAttributeCode("Attribute Code");
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckFilterImage(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE MoveUp@1000000000();
    VAR
      ToAttributeLink@1000000002 : Record 5447321;
      FromSorting@1000000001 : Integer;
      ToSorting@1000000000 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
      CASE Type OF
        Type::Item: BEGIN
          ToAttributeLink.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"No.",Sorting);
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,Type::Item);
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE("No.","No.");
        END;
        Type::Category: BEGIN
          ToAttributeLink.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"Shop Code","Language Code","Category Line No.",Sorting);
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,Type::Category);
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.","Category Line No.");
        END;
        Type::Shop: BEGIN
          ToAttributeLink.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"Shop Code",Sorting);
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,Type::Shop);
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
        END;
        Type::Template: BEGIN
          ToAttributeLink.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"No.",Sorting);
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,Type::Template);
          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE("No.","No.");
        END;
      END;
      ToAttributeLink.SETFILTER(Sorting,'<%1',Sorting);
      IF ToAttributeLink.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
        FromSorting := Sorting;
        ToSorting := ToAttributeLink.Sorting;
        Sorting := ToSorting;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
        ToAttributeLink.Sorting := FromSorting;
        ToAttributeLink.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;
      CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
    END;

          ToAttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
        END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ValueTypeOnAfterValidate@19028630();
    VAR
      Type@1000000000 : Integer;
    BEGIN

      Type := "Value Type";

      CASE Type OF
      0:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := TRUE;
          PercentageVisible := FALSE;
          "Item No.Visible" := FALSE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  TRUE;
        END;
      1:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := FALSE;
          PercentageVisible := TRUE;
          "Item No.Visible" := FALSE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  TRUE;
        END;
      2:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := FALSE;
          PercentageVisible := FALSE;
          "Item No.Visible" := TRUE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  TRUE;
        END;
      3:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := FALSE;
          PercentageVisible := FALSE;
          "Item No.Visible" := FALSE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  FALSE;
          "Category Coupon" := FALSE;
        END;
      4:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := FALSE;
          PercentageVisible := FALSE;
          "Item No.Visible" := FALSE;
          "Value CouponVisible" := FALSE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  FALSE;
        END;
      END;
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
      CurrPage.CouponSubform.FORM.FormatFields(Rec);

      IF "Value Type" = 3 THEN BEGIN
        "Value CouponVisible" := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        "Value CouponVisible" := FALSE;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE OnAfterGetCurrRecord@19077479();
    BEGIN

      xRec := Rec;
      CASE "Value Type" OF
      0:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := TRUE;
          PercentageVisible := FALSE;
          "Item No.Visible" := FALSE;
          "Value CouponVisible" := FALSE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  TRUE;
        END;
      1:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := FALSE;
          PercentageVisible := TRUE;
          "Item No.Visible" := FALSE;
          "Value CouponVisible" := FALSE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  TRUE;

        END;
      2:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := FALSE;
          PercentageVisible := FALSE;
          "Item No.Visible" := TRUE;
          "Value CouponVisible" := FALSE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  TRUE;
        END;
      3:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := FALSE;
          PercentageVisible := FALSE;
          "Item No.Visible" := FALSE;
          "Value CouponVisible" := TRUE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  FALSE;
          "Category Coupon" := FALSE;
        END;

      4:
        BEGIN
          AmountVisible := FALSE;
          PercentageVisible := FALSE;
          "Item No.Visible" := FALSE;
          "Value CouponVisible" := FALSE;
          CategoryCouponVisible :=  FALSE;
        END;

      END;
      CurrPage.CouponSubform.FORM.FormatFields(Rec);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ValueTypeOnInputChange@19034761(VAR Text@19028590 : Text[1024]);
    BEGIN
      Rec.Amount := 0;
      Rec.Percentage := 0;
      Rec."Item No." := '';
      ValueTypeOnAfterValidate;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.

    BEGIN
      CASE CouponHeader."Value Type" OF
        0:
          BEGIN
            "Customer NoVisible" := FALSE;
            AmountVisible := FALSE;
            "Amount LeftVisible" := FALSE;
            "Max No of UsageVisible" := TRUE;
          END;
        1:
          BEGIN
            "Customer NoVisible" := FALSE;
            AmountVisible := FALSE;
            "Amount LeftVisible" := FALSE;
            "Max No of UsageVisible" := TRUE;
          END;
        2:
          BEGIN
            "Customer NoVisible" := FALSE;
            AmountVisible := FALSE;
            "Amount LeftVisible" := FALSE;
            "Max No of UsageVisible" := TRUE;
          END;
        3:
          BEGIN
            "Customer NoVisible" := TRUE;
            AmountVisible := TRUE;
            "Amount LeftVisible" := TRUE;
            "Max No of UsageVisible" := FALSE;
          END;
        4:
          BEGIN
            "Customer NoVisible" := FALSE;
            AmountVisible := FALSE;
            "Amount LeftVisible" := FALSE;
            "Max No of UsageVisible" := TRUE;
          END;

      END;
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
    END;

                 CLEAR(CustomerNoFilter);
                 CLEAR(ShopCodeFilter);
                 CLEAR(LangCodeFilter);
                 CLEAR(LineNoFilter);
                 IF GETFILTER(Type)<>'' THEN BEGIN
                   TypeFilter := GETFILTER(Type);
                   SETRANGE(Type);
                   IF GETFILTER("Item No.")<>'' THEN BEGIN
                     ItemNoFilter := GETFILTER("Item No.");

                     SETRANGE("Item No.");

                     FILTERGROUP(2);
                     SETFILTER(Type,TypeFilter);
                     SETFILTER("Item No.",ItemNoFilter);
                     FILTERGROUP(0);
                   END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Customer No.")<>'' THEN BEGIN
                     CustomerNoFilter := GETFILTER("Customer No.");

                     SETRANGE("Customer No.");

                     FILTERGROUP(2);
                     SETFILTER(Type,TypeFilter);
                     SETFILTER("Customer No.",CustomerNoFilter);
                     FILTERGROUP(0);
                   END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Line No.")<>'' THEN BEGIN
                     LineNoFilter := GETFILTER("Line No.");
                     ShopCodeFilter := GETFILTER("Shop Code");
                     LangCodeFilter := GETFILTER("Language Code");

                     SETRANGE("Shop Code");
                     SETRANGE("Language Code");
                     SETRANGE("Line No.");

                     FILTERGROUP(2);
                     SETFILTER(Type,TypeFilter);
                     SETFILTER("Shop Code",ShopCodeFilter);
                     SETFILTER("Language Code",LangCodeFilter);
                     SETFILTER("Line No.",LineNoFilter);
                     FILTERGROUP(0);
                   END;
                 END;
               END;

  }
  CONTROLS

                ContainerType=ContentArea }

  {
    VAR
      TypeIsSequenceItem@1000000001 : Boolean INDATASET;
      TypeIsOrderOption@1000000000 : Boolean INDATASET;
      Text001@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Der Datensatz muss zur�ckgesetzt und alle Verkn�pftungen entfernt werden. Fortfahren?;ENU=The record has to be reset and all links removed. Continue?';
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000005 : Record 5447341;
      WebshopSubscriptionItemLink@1000000004 : Record 5447342;
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000003 : Record 5447343;
      CanSetLateEntryPossible@1000000006 : Boolean;

    PROCEDURE SetTypeIsOrderOption@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      IF Type = Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN
        TypeIsSequenceItem := FALSE;
        TypeIsOrderOption := TRUE;
        "Payment Type" := "Payment Type"::PerTurn;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        TypeIsSequenceItem := TRUE;
        TypeIsOrderOption := FALSE;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetTypeIsSequenceItem@1000000001();
    BEGIN
      IF Type = Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN BEGIN
        TypeIsOrderOption := FALSE;
        TypeIsSequenceItem := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        "Payment Type" := "Payment Type"::PerTurn;
        TypeIsOrderOption := TRUE;
        TypeIsSequenceItem := FALSE;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE SetCanSetLateEntryPossible@1000000003();
    BEGIN
      IF (Type = Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem)
         AND ("Start Type" = "Start Type"::Join)
         AND ("Payment Type" = "Payment Type"::Once)  THEN BEGIN
        CanSetLateEntryPossible := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        CanSetLateEntryPossible := FALSE;
        IF "Allow Late Entry" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          "Allow Late Entry" := FALSE;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

  {
    VAR
      ContentText@1000000009 : Text[30];
      LogoText@1000000010 : Text[30];
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000006 : Codeunit 5447301;
      WebshopWebform@1000000002 : Page 5447366;
      Type@1000000001 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2';
      WebformType@1000000000 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection,ShippingOption,PaymentOption,ShippingOptionIcon,PaymentOptionIcon';
      Text001@1000000013 : TextConst 'DEU=Logo gel�scht;ENU=Logo deleted';
      Text002@1000000014 : TextConst 'DEU=Beschreibung gel�scht;ENU=Description deleted';
      Text007@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte zuerst das n�tige Steuerelement installieren.;ENU=Please install needed Client AddIn first';
      YesText@1000000008 : TextConst 'DEU=Ja';
      NoText@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=Nein';
      MoveUpVisible@1000000012 : Boolean INDATASET;
      MoveDownVisible@1000000011 : Boolean INDATASET;

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShippingOption(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::ShippingOption);
      WebshopWebform.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckShippingOption(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");

      IF Content THEN
        ContentText := YesText
      ELSE
        ContentText := NoText;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditIcon@1000000001();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShippingOption(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      COMMIT;

      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::ShippingOptionIcon);
      WebshopWebform.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckShippingOptionIcon(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);

      IF Logo  THEN
        LogoText := YesText
      ELSE
        LogoText := NoText;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

      ContentText@1000000007 : Text[30];
      LogoText@1000000006 : Text[30];

      WebshopWebform@1000000002 : Page 5447366;
      Type@1000000001 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2';
      WebformType@1000000000 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection,ShippingOption,PaymentOption,ShippingOptionIcon,PaymentOptionIcon';
      Text007@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte zuerst das n�tige Steuerelement installieren.;ENU=Please install needed Client AddIn first';
      YesText@1000000009 : TextConst 'DEU=Ja';
      NoText@1000000008 : TextConst 'DEU=Nein';
      MoveUpVisible@1000000012 : Boolean INDATASET;
      MoveDownVisible@1000000011 : Boolean INDATASET;

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShippingOption(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::ShippingOption);
      WebshopWebform.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckShippingOption(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");

      IF Content THEN
        ContentText := YesText
      ELSE
        ContentText := NoText;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditIcon@1000000001();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShippingOption(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      COMMIT;

      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::ShippingOptionIcon);
      WebshopWebform.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckShippingOptionIcon(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);

      IF Logo  THEN
        LogoText := YesText
      ELSE
        LogoText := NoText;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");

      IF Content THEN
        ContentText := YesText
      ELSE
        ContentText := NoText;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditIcon@1000000001();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdatePaymentOption(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      COMMIT;

      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::PaymentOptionIcon);
      WebshopWebform.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckPaymentOptionIcon(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);

      IF Logo  THEN
        LogoText := YesText
      ELSE
        LogoText := NoText;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Page 5447366 Webshop Webform
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=04.08.17;
    Time=09:32:13;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    Editable=No;
    CaptionML=[DEU=Webshop Webform;
               ENU=Webshop Webform];
    InsertAllowed=No;
    DeleteAllowed=No;
    ModifyAllowed=No;
    LinksAllowed=No;
    PageType=NavigatePage;
    ShowFilter=No;
    OnOpenPage=BEGIN
                 WebshopSetup.GET;

                 DownloadEnabled := FALSE;
                 DeleteEnabled := FALSE;
                 PrevEnabled := FALSE;
                 NextEnabled := FALSE;

                 UnsavedChanges := FALSE;
                 SaveRequested := FALSE;
                 UrlLoaded := FALSE;
                 IF RecordCode <> '' THEN
                   CurrPage.CAPTION := CurrPage.CAPTION + ' - ' + RecordCode;

                 RANDOMIZE();

                 Request :=
                   STRSUBSTNO('%1webforms/webform.php?legacy=1&pass=%2&company=%3',
                     WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector",
                     WebshopSetup."HTTP Password",
                     WebshopManagement.URLEncode(COMPANYNAME));
                 Request +=
                   STRSUBSTNO('&rand=%1&nav_lang=%2&rtc=TRUE',
                     RANDOM(2000),
                     GLOBALLANGUAGE);

                 //Textedit
                 IF WebformType = WebformType::TextModul THEN BEGIN
                   Request +=
                     STRSUBSTNO('&code=%1&webform=TextModul&webformtype=Editor',
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(RecordCode));
                 END;

                 IF WebformType = WebformType::ItemDesc THEN BEGIN
                   Request +=
                     STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&item_no=%3&line_no=%4&webform=ItemDescription&webformtype=Editor',
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ItemNo),
                       LineNo);
                 END;

                 IF WebformType = WebformType::CategoryDesc THEN BEGIN
                   Request +=
                     STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&type=%4&webform=CategoryDesc&webformtype=Editor',
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
                       LineNo,
                       FORMAT(DescriptionType,0,'<Standard Format,2>'));
                 END;

                 IF WebformType = WebformType::ShippingOption THEN BEGIN
                   Request +=
                     STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&webform=ShippingOption&webformtype=Editor',
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
                       LineNo);
                 END;

                 IF WebformType = WebformType::PaymentOption THEN BEGIN
                   Request +=
                     STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&webform=PaymentOption&webformtype=Editor',
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
                       LineNo);
                 END;

                 //Marketplace

                 IF WebformType = WebformType::Marketplace THEN BEGIN
                   Request +=
                     STRSUBSTNO(
                 '&webform=Marketplace&webformtype=Marketplace&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&marketplace_type=%3&parent_id=%4&root_only%5',
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(FORMAT(MarketplaceType)),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(FORMAT(ParentId)),
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(FORMAT(RootOnly)),
                       FORMAT(AttachmentNo));
                   SaveRequested := TRUE;
                 END;

                 //Upload
                 IF WebformType = WebformType::TxtModAttac THEN BEGIN
                   Request +=
                     STRSUBSTNO('&text_module_code=%1&webform=TxtModAttac&webformtype=Upload&shop_code=%2&attachment_no=%3',
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(RecordCode),
                       WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code",
                       FORMAT(AttachmentNo));
                   SaveRequested := TRUE;
                 END;

                 IF WebformType = WebformType::ItemFile THEN BEGIN
                   Request +=
                     STRSUBSTNO('&webform=ItemFile&webformtype=Upload&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&item_no=%3&type=%4&line_no=%5',
                       WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),

                       ItemNo,
                       FORMAT(ItemFileType,0,'<Standard Format,2>'),
                       LineNo);
                   SaveRequested := TRUE;
                 END;

                 IF WebformType = WebformType::AttributeIcon THEN BEGIN

  {
    VAR
      ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[30];
      WebshopItem@1000000001 : Record 5447304;

    PROCEDURE SetItemNo@1000000002(ParamItemNo@1000000000 : Code[30]);
    VAR
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionItemLink@1000000002 : Record 5447342;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000003 : Record 5447341;
    BEGIN
      ItemNo := ParamItemNo;
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETRANGE("Item No. Subscription Item",ParamItemNo);
      Rec.DELETEALL(FALSE);
      IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        Rec := WebshopSubscriptionHeader;
        Rec.INSERT(FALSE);
        CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.RESET;
        WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.",ParamItemNo);
        IF WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscriptionItemLink."Subscription Code") AND
            (NOT Rec.GET(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code)) THEN BEGIN
            Rec := WebshopSubscriptionHeader;
            Rec.INSERT(FALSE);
          END;
        UNTIL WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.NEXT = 0;
        CurrPage.UPDATE(FALSE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopItem@1000000000(ParamWebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    BEGIN
      WebshopItem := ParamWebshopItem;
      SetItemNo(WebshopItem."Item No.");
    END;

    BEGIN
    {

    }
    END.

      TMPCampaignHeader@1000000001 : TEMPORARY Record 5447345;
      EditCampaignImage@1000000002 : Page 5447384;
      WebshopManagement@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447300;
      ThisRecRef@1000000000 : RecordRef;

    BEGIN

      CASE ParamField OF
        ParamField::IconCondItems:    ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(60);

      WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest();

    LOCAL PROCEDURE EditImage@1000000000(ParamField@1000000004 : 'IconCondItems,BannerCondItems,IconActItems,BannerActItems');
    VAR
      TMPCampaignHeader@1000000001 : TEMPORARY Record 5447345;
      EditCampaignImage@1000000002 : Page 5447384;
      WebshopManagement@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447300;
      ThisRecRef@1000000000 : RecordRef;
      ThisFieldRef@1000000005 : FieldRef;
    BEGIN
      ThisRecRef.GETTABLE(Rec);

      CASE ParamField OF
        ParamField::IconCondItems:    ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(60);
        ParamField::BannerCondItems:  ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(61);
        ParamField::IconActItems:     ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(63);
        ParamField::BannerActItems:   ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(64);
      END;

      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest();
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateCampaignByCode(Code);
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::CampaignImages);
      WebshopWebform.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebform.SetRecordCode(Code);
      WebshopWebform.SetWebFieldName(ParamField);
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckCampaignImage(Rec,ThisFieldRef);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE DeleteImage@1000000001(ParamField@1000000000 : 'IconCondItems,BannerCondItems,IconActItems,BannerActItems');
    VAR
      ThisRecRef@1000000002 : RecordRef;
      ThisFieldRef@1000000001 : FieldRef;
      WebshopManagement@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447300;
    BEGIN
      ThisRecRef.GETTABLE(Rec);

      CASE ParamField OF
        ParamField::IconCondItems:    ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(60);
        ParamField::BannerCondItems:  ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(61);
        ParamField::IconActItems:     ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(63);
        ParamField::BannerActItems:   ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(64);
      END;

      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest();
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateCampaignByCode(Code);
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
      COMMIT;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteCampaignImage(Rec,ThisFieldRef);
      CurrPage.UPDATE;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE EnableFields@1000000007(CampaignFieldType@1000000000 : 'Condition,Action';CampaignFieldName@1000000001 : 'CondSumAmntAllItemsGTE,CondSumAmntSingItemGTE,CondSumAmntEachItemGTE,CondNodiffItemsGTE,CondTotalItemQtyGTE,CondQtySingleItemGTE,CondQtyEachItemGTE,CondBasketTotalGTE,DiscountAmntappliesto,DiscountAmnt,DiscountPercentappliesto,DiscountPercent,DiscountappliestQtymax,TypeFreeItem,QtyFreeItem,AlternativeShippingOption';Used@1000000002 : Boolean);
    BEGIN

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Condition THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::CondSumAmntAllItemsGTE THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllCondFields;
            CondSumAmntAllItemsGTEEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllCondFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Condition THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::CondBasketTotalGTE THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllCondFields;
            CondBasketTotalGTEEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllCondFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Condition THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::CondNodiffItemsGTE THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllCondFields;
            CondNodiffItemsGTEEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllCondFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Condition THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::CondQtyEachItemGTE THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllCondFields;
            CondQtyEachItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllCondFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Condition THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::CondQtySingleItemGTE THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllCondFields;
            CondQtySingleItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllCondFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Condition THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::CondSumAmntEachItemGTE THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllCondFields;
            CondSumAmntEachItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllCondFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Condition THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::CondSumAmntSingItemGTE THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllCondFields;
            CondSumAmntSingItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllCondFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Condition THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::CondTotalItemQtyGTE THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllCondFields;
            CondTotalItemQtyGTEEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllCondFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Action THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::DiscountAmnt THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllActionFields;
            DiscountAmntEnabled := TRUE;
            DiscountAmntappliestoEnabled := TRUE;
            DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := TRUE;
            IF "Discount Amnt. applies to" = 0 THEN
              "Discount Amnt. applies to" := 1;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF "Discount Amnt. applies to" = 0 THEN
              EnableAllActionFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Action THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::DiscountAmntappliesto THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllActionFields;
            DiscountAmntEnabled := TRUE;
            DiscountAmntappliestoEnabled := TRUE;
            DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF "Discount applies to Qty. (max)" <> 0 THEN
              "Discount applies to Qty. (max)" := 0;
            EnableAllActionFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Action THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::DiscountPercent THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllActionFields;
            DiscountPercentEnabled := TRUE;
            DiscountPercentappliestoEnable := TRUE;
            DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := TRUE;
            IF "Discount Percent applies to" = 0 THEN
              "Discount Percent applies to" := 1;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF "Discount Percent applies to" = 0 THEN
              EnableAllActionFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Action THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::DiscountPercentappliesto THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllActionFields;
            DiscountPercentEnabled := TRUE;
            DiscountPercentappliestoEnable := TRUE;
            DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF "Discount applies to Qty. (max)" <> 0 THEN
              "Discount applies to Qty. (max)" := 0;
            EnableAllActionFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Action THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::DiscountappliestQtymax THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllActionFields;
            DiscountAmntAppliesTo := "Discount Amnt. applies to";
            DiscountPercentAppliesTo := "Discount Percent applies to";

            IF DiscountAmntAppliesTo = 0 THEN BEGIN
              DiscountPercentEnabled := TRUE;
              DiscountPercentappliestoEnable := TRUE;
              DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := TRUE;
            END;

            IF DiscountPercentAppliesTo = 0 THEN BEGIN
              DiscountAmntEnabled := TRUE;
              DiscountAmntappliestoEnabled := TRUE;
              DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := TRUE;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF ("Discount Percent applies to" = 0) AND ("Discount Amnt. applies to" = 0) THEN
              EnableAllActionFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Action THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::TypeFreeItem THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllActionFields;
            TypeFreeItemEnabled := TRUE;
            QtyFreeItemEnabled := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            "Qty. Free Item" := 0;
            EnableAllActionFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Action THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::QtyFreeItem THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllActionFields;
            TypeFreeItemEnabled := TRUE;
            QtyFreeItemEnabled := TRUE;
            IF "Type Free Item" = 0 THEN
              "Type Free Item" := 1;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF "Type Free Item" <> 0 THEN
              "Type Free Item" := 0;
              EnableAllActionFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF CampaignFieldType = CampaignFieldType::Action THEN BEGIN
        IF CampaignFieldName = CampaignFieldName::AlternativeShippingOption THEN BEGIN
          IF Used THEN BEGIN
            DisableAllActionFields;
            AlternativeShippingOptionEnabl := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            EnableAllActionFields;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE EnableAllCondFields@1000000011();
    BEGIN
      CondSumAmntAllItemsGTEEnabled := TRUE;
      CondSumAmntSingItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
      CondSumAmntEachItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
      CondNodiffItemsGTEEnabled := TRUE;
      CondTotalItemQtyGTEEnabled := TRUE;
      CondQtySingleItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
      CondQtyEachItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
      CondBasketTotalGTEEnabled := TRUE;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE EnableAllActionFields@1000000012();
    BEGIN
      DiscountAmntappliestoEnabled := TRUE;
      DiscountAmntEnabled := TRUE;
      DiscountPercentappliestoEnable := TRUE;
      DiscountPercentEnabled := TRUE;
      DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := TRUE;
      TypeFreeItemEnabled := TRUE;
      QtyFreeItemEnabled := TRUE;
      AlternativeShippingOptionEnabl := TRUE;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE DisableAllCondFields@1000000013();
    BEGIN
      CondSumAmntAllItemsGTEEnabled := FALSE;
      CondSumAmntSingItemGTEEnabled := FALSE;
      CondSumAmntEachItemGTEEnabled := FALSE;
      CondNodiffItemsGTEEnabled := FALSE;
      CondTotalItemQtyGTEEnabled := FALSE;
      CondQtySingleItemGTEEnabled := FALSE;
      CondQtyEachItemGTEEnabled := FALSE;
      CondBasketTotalGTEEnabled := FALSE;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE DisableAllActionFields@1000000014();
    BEGIN
      DiscountAmntappliestoEnabled := FALSE;
      DiscountAmntEnabled := FALSE;
      DiscountPercentappliestoEnable := FALSE;
      DiscountPercentEnabled := FALSE;
      DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := FALSE;
      TypeFreeItemEnabled := FALSE;
      QtyFreeItemEnabled := FALSE;
      AlternativeShippingOptionEnabl := FALSE;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckCondFields@1000000002();
    BEGIN
      CondFieldsUsed := FALSE;

      IF "Cond. Sum Amnt. All Items GTE" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        CondSumAmntAllItemsGTEEnabled := TRUE;
        CondFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Cond. Sum Amnt. Sing. Item GTE" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        CondSumAmntSingItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
        CondFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Cond. No. diff. Items GTE" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        CondNodiffItemsGTEEnabled := TRUE;
        CondFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Cond. Total Item Qty. GTE" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        CondTotalItemQtyGTEEnabled := TRUE;
        CondFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Cond. Qty. Single Item GTE" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        CondQtySingleItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
        CondFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Cond. Basket Total GTE" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        CondBasketTotalGTEEnabled := TRUE;
        CondFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Cond. Sum Amnt. Each Item GTE" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        CondSumAmntEachItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
        CondFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Cond. Qty. Each Item GTE" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        CondQtyEachItemGTEEnabled := TRUE;
        CondFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF NOT CondFieldsUsed THEN
        EnableAllCondFields;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckActionFields@1000000003();
    BEGIN
      ActionFieldsUsed := FALSE;

      IF ("Discount Amnt." <> 0) OR ("Discount Amnt. applies to" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
        DiscountAmntEnabled := TRUE;
        ActionFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Discount Amnt. applies to" <> "Discount Amnt. applies to"::"0" THEN BEGIN
        DiscountAmntappliestoEnabled := TRUE;
        ActionFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF ("Discount Percent" <> 0) OR ("Discount Percent applies to" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
        DiscountPercentEnabled := TRUE;
        ActionFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Discount Percent applies to" <> "Discount Percent applies to"::"0" THEN BEGIN
        DiscountPercentappliestoEnable := TRUE;
        ActionFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF ("Type Free Item" <> 0) OR ("Qty. Free Item" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
        TypeFreeItemEnabled := TRUE;
        QtyFreeItemEnabled := TRUE;
        ActionFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF "Alternative Shipping Option" THEN BEGIN
        AlternativeShippingOptionEnabl := TRUE;
        ActionFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF ("Discount applies to Qty. (max)" <> 0) OR ("Discount Amnt. applies to" <> 0) OR ("Discount Percent applies to" <> 0) THEN
      BEGIN
        DiscountappliestQtymaxEnabled := TRUE;
        ActionFieldsUsed := TRUE;
      END;

      IF NOT ActionFieldsUsed THEN
        EnableAllActionFields;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Page 5447384 Webshop Edit Campaign Images
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=30.06.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    SourceTable=Table5447345;
    OnOpenPage=BEGIN
                 WebshopSetup.GET;
               END;

    OnNextRecord=BEGIN
                   EXIT;
                 END;

    OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                       WebFieldName@1000000000 : Text[80];
                       ParamField@1000000001 : 'IconCondItems,BannerCondItems,IconActItems,BannerActItems';
                     BEGIN
                       // Bild in Webform bearbeiten

                       CASE Field OF
                         ParamField::IconCondItems:   WebFieldName := 'icon_condition_items';
                         ParamField::BannerCondItems: WebFieldName := 'banner_condition_items';
                         ParamField::IconActItems:    WebFieldName := 'icon_action_items';
                         ParamField::BannerActItems:  WebFieldName := 'banner_action_items';
                       END;


                       TESTFIELD(Code);
                       WebshopSetup.TESTFIELD("HTTP Adress Connector");
                       RANDOMIZE();
                       Request :=
                         STRSUBSTNO(
                       '%1webforms/edit_campaign_image.php?pass=%2&company=%3&shop_code=%4&lang_code=%5&nav_lang=%6&code=%7&field=%8&rand=%9',
                           WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector",
                           WebshopSetup."HTTP Password",
                           WebshopManagement.URLEncode(COMPANYNAME),
                           WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
                           WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LangCode),
                           GLOBALLANGUAGE,
                           Code,
                           WebFieldName,
                           RANDOM(2000));
                     END;

  }
  CONTROLS
  {
    { 1000000001;0;Container;
                ContainerType=ContentArea }

    { 1000000000;1;Field  ;
                Name=RequestField;
                SourceExpr=Request;
                ControlAddIn=[DynamicCommerce.MysydeShop.Connect;PublicKeyToken=eb1e70a97bfaeec6] }

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopSetup@1000000000 : Record 5447300;
